codex
# Sol stage-2 final adjudication

Occurrence: `occ_critique_r7_superfixer_retry_20260806_v4_14834310cdddb1f2b0eed77e`  
Logical occurrence digest: `sha256:14834310cdddb1f2b0eed77efb40585bd2d7ce1e73cd64497bad74246bd5c856`  
Disposition: **Horizon A executable; no external gate**

## A. Adjudicated root cause

### First broken contract

The evaluator/gate-to-finalize critique-custody contract persisted both:

- `CF-0B506E1EDCD92E90C192`
- `CF-B67C1E37D72114DDCF70`

as `accepted_tradeoff` with traceable fixed-plan mutations, but without `verified=true`, a compatible `gate_resolution`, or a surviving `gate_carry` entry. The bound finalize consumer requires that missing envelope and therefore raises `critique_finding_unresolved`.

The proven 16-line branch:

```python
if status == "accepted_tradeoff" and gate_expected and fixed_claim:
    # resolve as verified_plan_mutation
```

restores the missing semantic equivalence. It resolves all 95 findings under both independent candidates.

### Deeper issue

The pipeline has no single versioned contract connecting:

- evaluator/flag resolution,
- gate carry,
- finalize resolution policy,
- runtime content identity,
- recovery-attempt identity, and
- repair authority.

Consequently, semantically resolved findings can become unreadable to finalize, while the repair producer can also be unable to represent the failed phase because its required F01 `attempt` field was never canonically persisted.

### Adherence versus missing structure

The original execution adhered to its then-bound runtime and failed closed as implemented. The v5 `PROCEED` gate remains valid but is not finalize success.

Missing structure comprises:

- compatible accepted-tradeoff carry semantics;
- the finalize-consumer relaxation;
- an A→fixed-descendant runtime-rebind event;
- a canonical finalize phase-attempt record for `CustodyTargetKey.attempt`;
- a finalize-specific request, grant, claim/epoch, and WBC attempt/effect;
- automatic terminalization of the obsolete revise request;
- admission-time producer/consumer compatibility checking.

The absence of a live runner, repair request, claim, or lease is clearance for recovery, not itself authority.

### Canonical ownership

The semantic defect belongs to the Megaplan critique-custody contract centered on:

- `arnold_pipelines/megaplan/orchestration/critique_custody.py`
- evaluator/flag and gate-carry producers
- `arnold_pipelines/megaplan/handlers/finalize.py`

The missing F01 `attempt` is canonically owned by writer `megaplan.phase_wbc.finalize`, through `arnold_pipelines.megaplan.custody.phase_wbc.activate_phase_wbc`, persisted append-only in the plan-local `.phase_wbc_attempts.sqlite3`.

Run Authority, Custody, WBC, runtime binding, the ordinary fixer, and notification custody enforce the recovery but do not replace those owners.

## B. Horizon A

`agent_actionable: true`  
`immediate_route: repair_control_plane_then_migrate`  
`external_gate: null`

This route preserves the logical occurrence. It creates a new execution attempt under the same digest; it does not rewrite the three failed attempts or mint a replacement occurrence from labels.

### Preconditions

Before the first effect, reread and require:

- Chain remains `blocked` at milestone index `0`, current plan `cl2-wbc-backed-ledger-20260805-2140`, `completed=[]`.
- Plan remains blocked with `resume_cursor.phase=finalize`.
- Gate v5 remains `PROCEED`, SHA-256 `9df9582599f9fe096df87ab1ff23665a9e116ea959deae3e3102feab9dbead3b`.
- The failure set remains exactly the two named findings.
- There is no live runner, fresh lease, finalize request, repair claim, WBC recovery attempt/effect, or notification intent/effect.
- The named runtime checkout is clean and remains a descendant of runtime A.
- The operator’s v4 charge remains binding.

This is a guarded runtime cutover only. It must not move the chain cursor or rewrite the chain-spec binding.

### Canonical attempt and ordered recovery path

1. Through the supported recovery producer, allocate a new finalize invocation and call:

   ```python
   arnold_pipelines.megaplan.custody.phase_wbc.activate_phase_wbc(
       state=state,
       plan_dir=plan_dir,
       step="finalize",
       agent="ordinary-fixer",
   )
   ```

   Use its append-only `attempt_id` from `.phase_wbc_attempts.sqlite3` as `CustodyTargetKey.attempt`.

2. If blocked-state recovery cannot legally enter that API, repair that internal producer/re-entry seam in the same authorized editable runtime. Test and rebind the new descendant. Do not derive `attempt` from events, labels, PIDs, liveness, or prose, and do not edit SQLite or `state.json` directly.

3. Build the full identity using `build_normalized_repair_identity`, then call `enqueue_occurrence_bound_repair_request`. The F01 target must include the canonical finalize attempt plus environment, session, chain, plan revision, phase, task, failure kind, blocker/result hash, fence, and chain identity. The normalized envelope must additionally bind run revision/incarnation, coordinator attempt, WBC attempt reference, grant, lease, and custody epoch.

4. Run Authority persists one decision for this request and occurrence. A denial terminates the attempt without downstream effects. An allow decision materializes the exact grant.

5. Custody calls `claim_singleton_occurrence`, creating exactly one claim, lease, and epoch bound to that grant and logical digest.

6. WBC reserves and starts exactly one recovery attempt and one occurrence-scoped effect key.

7. `delegate_to_simple_fixer` invokes the canonical `CanonicalRunner`. The ordinary fixer follows the existing `resume_cursor` and re-enters only `finalize`.

8. Finalize writes critique clearance, runs the real validator, admits the task/result envelopes, commits publication under WBC/CAS, terminalizes WBC success, releases Custody, and lets the ordinary chain driver advance the cursor and milestone.

These are internal containment points, not evidence of an external gate. Preserve the evidence delta and retry only after the named internal seam has been repaired and its focused tests pass. Return to Sol only if a new contract conflict or genuine external approval/provider dependency is discovered.

## C. Horizon B

`epic_update_required: true`  
`agent_actionable: false`

### Complete category fix

The first broken contract is the incompatible, unversioned gate-to-finalize resolution envelope. The missed backstop is twofold:

- gate/runtime admission did not prove that the persisted resolution state was consumable by the exact bound finalize policy;
- repair admission could not canonically allocate the finalize attempt required by F01 after the ordinary runner had stopped.

The canonical owner remains the Megaplan critique-custody contract.

### Contract and schema

Add a content-addressed `arnold.megaplan.recovery_admission.v1` envelope binding:

- session, chain, plan, milestone, logical occurrence, and blocker family;
- finding/flag state, fixed claim, source/addressed/current plan versions, and mutation evidence;
- gate action, rationale, evidence, complete carry history, and gate hash;
- finalize-policy version and validator receipt;
- prior/target runtime revisions, content hashes, editable/import provenance, and rebind history;
- canonical phase-attempt ID;
- request, Run Authority grant, Custody claim/epoch/fence/lease;
- WBC attempt/effect and terminal outcome;
- notification intent/effect key.

Admission should fail unless referenced records hash back to this envelope.

### Concrete implementation surface

Update and test:

- `orchestration/critique_custody.py`
- evaluator/flag verification and gate-carry producers
- `handlers/finalize.py`
- `orchestration/finalize_authority.py`
- `custody/phase_wbc.py`
- `custody/wbc_runtime.py`
- `custody/contracts.py`
- `cloud/repair_requests.py`
- `cloud/wrappers/repair_delegation.py`
- `cloud/simple_fixer.py`
- `chain/execution_binding.py`
- `cloud/runtime_provenance.py`
- `cloud/runtime_cutover.py`
- Run Authority and Custody stores
- `cloud/repair_effect_ledger.py`
- notification intent/outbox/delivery custody
- observer/work-ledger transition reporting.

Required tests include:

- carried `accepted_tradeoff` with a traceable descendant mutation;
- missing or contradictory carry rejected before `PROCEED`;
- producer/consumer compatibility against the bound finalize policy;
- canonical recovery allocation of a missing finalize attempt;
- request/attempt crash and retry dedupe;
- atomic runtime-rebind CAS and stale provenance rejection;
- stale fence/lease/epoch/grant and duplicate claim rejection;
- finalize publication crash recovery;
- exactly-once notification admission/effect;
- R7 and bigbang failure-family replay.

### Migration, observability, and rollout

Migration must append receipts without rewriting historical plan, gate, fault, chain, request, or WBC records. Backfill only where complete provenance exists; otherwise retain explicit unknown/quarantined status. Terminalize self-recovered orphan requests through a reviewed migration rule.

Add metrics for missing carry, unresolved-finding family, missing phase attempt, zero-authority rejection, runtime drift, rebind CAS conflict, duplicate claims, orphan WBC attempts, missing phase terminal evidence, and notification intent/effect divergence.

Roll out through historical shadow replay, dual-write/compare, bounded canary admission, enforcement for new occurrences, then migration of eligible blocked occurrences. Rollback disables new admission but preserves append-only identities and effects.

### Epic crosswalk

Update `.megaplan/initiatives/critique-ledger-accountability-v3-r7-20260805`:

- `README.md`: recovery-admission contract and owners.
- `NORTHSTAR.md`: category-level exactly-once recovery invariant.

- Add workstreams for gate/finalize compatibility, Run Authority/Custody/WBC admission, runtime rebind, repair lifecycle closure, observer correctness, and notification dedupe.

### Category closure proof

For both R7 and bigbang fixtures, replay the immutable evidence through the real validator and prove per logical recovery:

- exactly one occurrence;
- exactly one Custody claim/epoch;
- exactly one WBC attempt/effect reservation;
- exactly one terminal result;
- at most one deduplicated notification effect;
- no orphan request, grant, lease, claim, reservation, intent, or effect.

Inject crashes and retries at every producer, rebind, authority, custody, WBC, publication, release, and notification boundary.

The following remain **INDETERMINATE** pending category design:

- whether fixed `accepted_tradeoff` should always equal verified mutation or only under a versioned policy;
- exact-finding versus error-family category identity;
- whether every unsafe legacy occurrence must migrate to a linked child;
- whether selector/output declarations become mandatory;
- which legacy blocked states can safely receive CAS versions;
- the terminal notification conduit and admission policy.

## D. Overrides of prior Sol stage 2

The following v1 conclusions are overridden:

- **Quarantine-only Horizon A:** overridden. The v4 operator charge authorizes the editable-runtime repair, provenance, guarded rebind, and control-plane repair loop.
- **Runtime B requires additional external approval:** overridden. Runtime identity remains a required CAS/provenance boundary, but the A/B distinction is not itself an external gate.
- **Missing finalize CAS/idempotency proves re-entry unauthorized:** overridden as a terminal conclusion. It remains a stop gate handled by a new canonical phase attempt, Custody claim, and WBC attempt/effect.
- **Selector/output uncertainty blocks this occurrence:** overridden for Horizon A. It remains Horizon B work unless the actual validator rejects the recovery envelopes.
- **Sibling/category recurrence blocks occurrence recovery:** overridden. It mandates Horizon B and containment, not indefinite quarantine of this proven occurrence-level fix.
- **Missing historical request/attempt records require human authorization:** overridden. They are internal control-plane defects. The attempt must be minted through the canonical phase-WBC owner or that owner repaired.
- **Runtime A must remain active because it is recorded:** overridden after successful guarded `runtime-rebind`. Direct unrecorded substitution remains forbidden.
- **No same-occurrence route exists:** overridden. The same logical occurrence may proceed through a new, exactly-once execution attempt.

# Sol stage-2 final adjudication

Occurrence: `occ_critique_r7_superfixer_retry_20260806_v4_14834310cdddb1f2b0eed77e`  
Logical occurrence digest: `sha256:14834310cdddb1f2b0eed77efb40585bd2d7ce1e73cd64497bad74246bd5c856`  
Disposition: **Horizon A executable; no external gate**

## A. Adjudicated root cause

### First broken contract

The evaluator/gate-to-finalize critique-custody contract persisted both:

- `CF-0B506E1EDCD92E90C192`
- `CF-B67C1E37D72114DDCF70`

as `accepted_tradeoff` with traceable fixed-plan mutations, but without `verified=true`, a compatible `gate_resolution`, or a surviving `gate_carry` entry. The bound finalize consumer requires that missing envelope and therefore raises `critique_finding_unresolved`.

The proven 16-line branch:

```python
if status == "accepted_tradeoff" and gate_expected and fixed_claim:
    # resolve as verified_plan_mutation
```

restores the missing semantic equivalence. It resolves all 95 findings under both independent candidates.

### Deeper issue

The pipeline has no single versioned contract connecting:

- evaluator/flag resolution,
- gate carry,
- finalize resolution policy,
- runtime content identity,
- recovery-attempt identity, and
- repair authority.

Consequently, semantically resolved findings can become unreadable to finalize, while the repair producer can also be unable to represent the failed phase because its required F01 `attempt` field was never canonically persisted.

### Adherence versus missing structure

The original execution adhered to its then-bound runtime and failed closed as implemented. The v5 `PROCEED` gate remains valid but is not finalize success.

Missing structure comprises:

- compatible accepted-tradeoff carry semantics;
- the finalize-consumer relaxation;
- an A→fixed-descendant runtime-rebind event;
- a canonical finalize phase-attempt record for `CustodyTargetKey.attempt`;
- a finalize-specific request, grant, claim/epoch, and WBC attempt/effect;
- automatic terminalization of the obsolete revise request;
- admission-time producer/consumer compatibility checking.

The absence of a live runner, repair request, claim, or lease is clearance for recovery, not itself authority.

### Canonical ownership

The semantic defect belongs to the Megaplan critique-custody contract centered on:

- `arnold_pipelines/megaplan/orchestration/critique_custody.py`
- evaluator/flag and gate-carry producers
- `arnold_pipelines/megaplan/handlers/finalize.py`

The missing F01 `attempt` is canonically owned by writer `megaplan.phase_wbc.finalize`, through `arnold_pipelines.megaplan.custody.phase_wbc.activate_phase_wbc`, persisted append-only in the plan-local `.phase_wbc_attempts.sqlite3`.

Run Authority, Custody, WBC, runtime binding, the ordinary fixer, and notification custody enforce the recovery but do not replace those owners.

## B. Horizon A

`agent_actionable: true`  
`immediate_route: repair_control_plane_then_migrate`  
`external_gate: null`

This route preserves the logical occurrence. It creates a new execution attempt under the same digest; it does not rewrite the three failed attempts or mint a replacement occurrence from labels.

### Preconditions

Before the first effect, reread and require:

- Chain remains `blocked` at milestone index `0`, current plan `cl2-wbc-backed-ledger-20260805-2140`, `completed=[]`.
- Plan remains blocked with `resume_cursor.phase=finalize`.
- Gate v5 remains `PROCEED`, SHA-256 `9df9582599f9fe096df87ab1ff23665a9e116ea959deae3e3102feab9dbead3b`.
- The failure set remains exactly the two named findings.
- There is no live runner, fresh lease, finalize request, repair claim, WBC recovery attempt/effect, or notification intent/effect.
- The named runtime checkout is clean and remains a descendant of runtime A.
- The operator’s v4 charge remains binding.

### Runtime descendant, installation, provenance, and rebind

In `/workspace/runtime-candidates/arnold-r7-fresh-child-20260805`:


Require `ok=true`, exact editable/import roots, and matching source/editable revision. Extract the resulting `TARGET_CONTENT_SHA256`, then invoke:

```bash
run_mp chain runtime-rebind \

  --project-dir /workspace/critique-ledger-accountability-v3-r7-launch-20260805/Arnold \

  --runtime-identity "$RUNTIME_IDENTITY_JSON" \
  --runtime-provenance-receipt "$RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_JSON"
```

This is a guarded runtime cutover only. It must not move the chain cursor or rewrite the chain-spec binding.

### Canonical attempt and ordered recovery path

1. Through the supported recovery producer, allocate a new finalize invocation and call:

   ```python
   arnold_pipelines.megaplan.custody.phase_wbc.activate_phase_wbc(
       state=state,
       plan_dir=plan_dir,
       step="finalize",
       agent="ordinary-fixer",
   )
   ```

   Use its append-only `attempt_id` from `.phase_wbc_attempts.sqlite3` as `CustodyTargetKey.attempt`.

2. If blocked-state recovery cannot legally enter that API, repair that internal producer/re-entry seam in the same authorized editable runtime. Test and rebind the new descendant. Do not derive `attempt` from events, labels, PIDs, liveness, or prose, and do not edit SQLite or `state.json` directly.

3. Build the full identity using `build_normalized_repair_identity`, then call `enqueue_occurrence_bound_repair_request`. The F01 target must include the canonical finalize attempt plus environment, session, chain, plan revision, phase, task, failure kind, blocker/result hash, fence, and chain identity. The normalized envelope must additionally bind run revision/incarnation, coordinator attempt, WBC attempt reference, grant, lease, and custody epoch.

4. Run Authority persists one decision for this request and occurrence. A denial terminates the attempt without downstream effects. An allow decision materializes the exact grant.

5. Custody calls `claim_singleton_occurrence`, creating exactly one claim, lease, and epoch bound to that grant and logical digest.

6. WBC reserves and starts exactly one recovery attempt and one occurrence-scoped effect key.

7. `delegate_to_simple_fixer` invokes the canonical `CanonicalRunner`. The ordinary fixer follows the existing `resume_cursor` and re-enters only `finalize`.

8. Finalize writes critique clearance, runs the real validator, admits the task/result envelopes, commits publication under WBC/CAS, terminalizes WBC success, releases Custody, and lets the ordinary chain driver advance the cursor and milestone.

### Stop gates and next retry condition

Stop the current effect on any identity drift, dirty/non-descendant runtime, provenance failure, rebind CAS refusal, live competing owner, duplicate request/claim/WBC key, unaccounted prior effect, stale fence/lease/epoch/grant, missing canonical attempt, failed regression, validator rejection, repeated custody error, new failure family, or missing terminal phase evidence.

These are internal containment points, not evidence of an external gate. Preserve the evidence delta and retry only after the named internal seam has been repaired and its focused tests pass. Return to Sol only if a new contract conflict or genuine external approval/provider dependency is discovered.

## C. Horizon B

`epic_update_required: true`  
`agent_actionable: false`

### Complete category fix

The first broken contract is the incompatible, unversioned gate-to-finalize resolution envelope. The missed backstop is twofold:

- gate/runtime admission did not prove that the persisted resolution state was consumable by the exact bound finalize policy;
- repair admission could not canonically allocate the finalize attempt required by F01 after the ordinary runner had stopped.

The canonical owner remains the Megaplan critique-custody contract.

### Contract and schema

Add a content-addressed `arnold.megaplan.recovery_admission.v1` envelope binding:

- session, chain, plan, milestone, logical occurrence, and blocker family;
- finding/flag state, fixed claim, source/addressed/current plan versions, and mutation evidence;
- gate action, rationale, evidence, complete carry history, and gate hash;
- finalize-policy version and validator receipt;
- prior/target runtime revisions, content hashes, editable/import provenance, and rebind history;
- canonical phase-attempt ID;
- request, Run Authority grant, Custody claim/epoch/fence/lease;
- WBC attempt/effect and terminal outcome;
- notification intent/effect key.

Admission should fail unless referenced records hash back to this envelope.

### Concrete implementation surface

Update and test:

- `orchestration/critique_custody.py`
- evaluator/flag verification and gate-carry producers
- `handlers/finalize.py`
- `orchestration/finalize_authority.py`
- `custody/phase_wbc.py`
- `custody/wbc_runtime.py`
- `custody/contracts.py`
- `cloud/repair_requests.py`
- `cloud/wrappers/repair_delegation.py`
- `cloud/simple_fixer.py`
- `chain/execution_binding.py`
- `cloud/runtime_provenance.py`
- `cloud/runtime_cutover.py`
- Run Authority and Custody stores
- `cloud/repair_effect_ledger.py`
- notification intent/outbox/delivery custody
- observer/work-ledger transition reporting.

Required tests include:

- carried `accepted_tradeoff` with a traceable descendant mutation;
- missing or contradictory carry rejected before `PROCEED`;
- producer/consumer compatibility against the bound finalize policy;
- canonical recovery allocation of a missing finalize attempt;
- request/attempt crash and retry dedupe;
- atomic runtime-rebind CAS and stale provenance rejection;
- stale fence/lease/epoch/grant and duplicate claim rejection;
- finalize publication crash recovery;
- exactly-once notification admission/effect;
- R7 and bigbang failure-family replay.

### Migration, observability, and rollout

Migration must append receipts without rewriting historical plan, gate, fault, chain, request, or WBC records. Backfill only where complete provenance exists; otherwise retain explicit unknown/quarantined status. Terminalize self-recovered orphan requests through a reviewed migration rule.

Add metrics for missing carry, unresolved-finding family, missing phase attempt, zero-authority rejection, runtime drift, rebind CAS conflict, duplicate claims, orphan WBC attempts, missing phase terminal evidence, and notification intent/effect divergence.

Roll out through historical shadow replay, dual-write/compare, bounded canary admission, enforcement for new occurrences, then migration of eligible blocked occurrences. Rollback disables new admission but preserves append-only identities and effects.

### Epic crosswalk

Update `.megaplan/initiatives/critique-ledger-accountability-v3-r7-20260805`:

- `README.md`: recovery-admission contract and owners.
- `NORTHSTAR.md`: category-level exactly-once recovery invariant.
- `UNFINISHED_WORK.md`: migration, runtime history, attempt recovery, and notification custody.
- `briefs/cl2-ledger-persistence-and-replay.md`: resolution envelope, phase-attempt ownership, and category replay.
- Add workstreams for gate/finalize compatibility, Run Authority/Custody/WBC admission, runtime rebind, repair lifecycle closure, observer correctness, and notification dedupe.

### Category closure proof

For both R7 and bigbang fixtures, replay the immutable evidence through the real validator and prove per logical recovery:

- exactly one occurrence;
- exactly one Custody claim/epoch;
- exactly one WBC attempt/effect reservation;
- exactly one terminal result;
- at most one deduplicated notification effect;
- no orphan request, grant, lease, claim, reservation, intent, or effect.

Inject crashes and retries at every producer, rebind, authority, custody, WBC, publication, release, and notification boundary.

The following remain **INDETERMINATE** pending category design:

- whether fixed `accepted_tradeoff` should always equal verified mutation or only under a versioned policy;
- exact-finding versus error-family category identity;
- whether every unsafe legacy occurrence must migrate to a linked child;
- whether selector/output declarations become mandatory;
- which legacy blocked states can safely receive CAS versions;
- the terminal notification conduit and admission policy.

## D. Overrides of prior Sol stage 2

The following v1 conclusions are overridden:

- **Quarantine-only Horizon A:** overridden. The v4 operator charge authorizes the editable-runtime repair, provenance, guarded rebind, and control-plane repair loop.
- **Runtime B requires additional external approval:** overridden. Runtime identity remains a required CAS/provenance boundary, but the A/B distinction is not itself an external gate.
- **Missing finalize CAS/idempotency proves re-entry unauthorized:** overridden as a terminal conclusion. It remains a stop gate handled by a new canonical phase attempt, Custody claim, and WBC attempt/effect.
- **Selector/output uncertainty blocks this occurrence:** overridden for Horizon A. It remains Horizon B work unless the actual validator rejects the recovery envelopes.
- **Sibling/category recurrence blocks occurrence recovery:** overridden. It mandates Horizon B and containment, not indefinite quarantine of this proven occurrence-level fix.
- **Missing historical request/attempt records require human authorization:** overridden. They are internal control-plane defects. The attempt must be minted through the canonical phase-WBC owner or that owner repaired.
- **Runtime A must remain active because it is recorded:** overridden after successful guarded `runtime-rebind`. Direct unrecorded substitution remains forbidden.
- **No same-occurrence route exists:** overridden. The same logical occurrence may proceed through a new, exactly-once execution attempt.

## E. `recovery_handoff.v1`

`handoff_id` is the SHA-256 of UTF-8 canonical JSON using sorted keys and comma/colon separators, with the self-referential `handoff_id` member omitted.

```json
{
  "schema": "arnold.superfixer.recovery_handoff.v1",

  "handoff_hash_basis": "sha256 of UTF-8 canonical JSON (sorted keys, separators comma/colon) with handoff_id omitted",
  "target": {
    "session": "critique-ledger-accountability-v3-r7-launch-20260805",
    "workspace": "/workspace/critique-ledger-accountability-v3-r7-launch-20260805/Arnold",
    "plan": "cl2-wbc-backed-ledger-20260805-2140",
```
